elements\nusing idx_t = uint64_t;\nusing val_t = double;\n\n// Maximum number of connections per element (for a 2D grid: 4 neighbors)\nconstexpr int MAX_CONNECTIONS = 8;\n\n// Material properties for energy transfer\nstruct Material {\n    val_t transfer_coeff;  // Energy transfer coefficient\n    val_t external_flow;   // External energy source/sink\n};\n\n// Static connectivity information for each element\nstruct ElementStatic {\n    idx_t material_idx;\n    idx_t num_connections;\n    idx_t connected_idx[MAX_CONNECTIONS];     // Indices of connected elements\n    val_t total_flux;\n};\n\n// World state\nstruct World {\n    std::vector<Material> materials;\n    std::vector<ElementStatic> elements_static;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic_swap;\n};\n\n// Material type IDs\nconstexpr idx_t DEFAULT_MAT_ID = 0;\nconstexpr idx_t INFLOW_MAT_ID = 1;\nconstexpr idx_t OUTFLOW_MAT_ID = 2;\n\n// Build a 2D square grid as an unstructured mesh\n// This represents computation on arbitrarily-shaped geometries\nvoid buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {\n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n    \n    // Initialize materials\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0});    // Default material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material\n    \n    // Allocate elements\n    world.elements_static.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(n_elems);\n    \n    // Initialize all elements with default material and zero energy\n    for (int i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n        world.elements_static[i].material_idx = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;\n        world.elements_static[i].num_connections = 0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n    }\n    \n    // Build connectivity: each element connects to its neighbors in 2D grid\n    for (int x = 0; x < n_elems_root; ++x) {\n        for (int y = 0; y < n_elems_root; ++y) {\n            const int idx = x * n_elems_root + y;\n            ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[idx];\n            \n            // Connect to neighbors (up, down, left, right)\n            const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};\n            \n            for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {\n                const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];\n                const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];\n                \n                // Check if neighbor is within bounds\n                if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 && ny < n_elems_root) {\n                    const int neighbor_idx = nx * n_elems_root + ny;\n                    elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;\n                    elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;\n                    elem.num_connections++;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics\n    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n}\n\n// Compute energy flux between two elements\ninline val_t computeFlux(const Material& mat, const ElementDynamic& this_elem,\n                        val_t connection_flux, const ElementDynamic& other_elem) {\n    return (other_elem.current_energy - this_elem.current_energy) * \n           mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {\n    const size_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();\n    \n    for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {\n        // Update all elements\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n            const ElementStatic& elem_static = world.elements_static[i];\n            const ElementDynamic& elem_dyn = world.elements_dynamic[i];\n            const Material& mat = world.materials[elem_static.material_idx];\n            \n            // Start with external flow\n            val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;\n            \n            // Add flux from all connected elements\n            for (idx_t j = 0; j < elem_static.num_connections; ++j) {\n                const idx_t neighbor_idx = elem_static.connected_flux[j], neighbor_dyn);\n            }\n            \n            // Update element state\n            ElementDynamic& elem_write = world.elements_dynamic_swap[i];\n            elem_write.current_energy = elem_dyn.current_energy + total_flux;\n            elem_write.total_flux = elem_dyn.total_flux + std::abs(total_flux);\n        }\n        \n        // Swap buffers\n        std::swap(world.elements_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic_swap);\n    }\n}\n\n// Validate simulation results\nbool validateResults(const World& world) {\n    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n    \n    for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {\n        energy_sum += elem.current_energy;\n        flux_sum += elem.total_flux;\n        energy_max = std::max(elem.current_energy, energy_max);\n        energy_min = std::min(elem.current_energy, energy_min);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Validation results:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  Energy sum: %.12f\\n\", energy_sum);\n    printf(\"  Flux sum: %.2f\\n\", flux_sum);\n    printf(\"  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", energy_min, energy_max);\n    \n    // Check for numerical issues\n    constexpr val_t energy_epsilon = 1e-8;\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {\n        printf(\"  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\\n\");\n        // Don't fail validation as this can happen with external flows\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\\n\");\n        return elements) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < elements.size(); ++i) {\n        // Simple hash combining energy and flux values\n        const uint64_t* e_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].current_energy);\n        const uint64_t* f_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].total_flux);\n        hash ^= (*e_ptr + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\n        hash ^= (*f_ptr + i) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;\n    }\n    return hash;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Grid size (NxN elements) (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of simulation iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable duration_ms = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start).count();\n    \n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration_ms);\n    \n    // Calculate performance metrics\n    const int n_measured_iters = std::max(n_iters - 1, 1);\n    const double time_per_iter = static_cast<double>(duration_ms) / n_measured_iters;\n    const double giga_elems_per_sec = (n_measured_iters * n_elems) / (duration_ms / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n    \n    // Approximate FLOPS: ~22 FLOPS per element per iteration (from reference)\n    const double gflops = giga_elems_per_sec * printf(\"  Performance: %.4f GFLOPS\\n\", gflops);\n    \n    // Compute hash for verification\n    const uint64_t hash = computeHash(world.elements_dynamic);\n    printf(\"  Result hash: %016lX\\n\", energyData.reserve(world.elements_dynamic.size());\n        for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {\n            energyData.push_back(elem.current_energy);\n        }\n }\n    }\n    \n    return 0;\n}\n------\n#pragma once\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <cinttypes>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include \"hash.hpp\"\n\n// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum\ntemplate <typename T>\nT kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {\n    T sum = 0.0;\n    T c = 0.0;\n    for (const auto& val : data) {\n        T y = val - c;\n        T t = sum + y;\n        c = (t - sum) - y;\n        sum = t;\n    }\n    return sum;\n}\n\n// Print results for floating-point arrays\ntemplate <typename T>\nvoid print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = \"Result\") {\n    if (data.empty()) {\n        printf(\"=== RESULTS ===\\n\");\n        printf(\"Name: %s\\n\", name);\n        printf(\"Elements: 0\\n\");\n        printf(\"=== END RESULTS ===\\n\");\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const size_t N = data.size();\n    \n    // Compute statistics\n    T sum = kahan_sum(data);\n    T min_val = data[0];\n    T <vector>\n\n#include <mpi.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\n// Types to represent unstructured mesh elements\nusing idx_t = uint64_t;\nusing val_t = double;\n\n// Maximum number of connections per element (for a 2D grid: 4 neighbors)\nconstexpr int MAX_CONNECTIONS = 8;\n\n// Material properties for energy transfer\nstruct Material {\n    val_t transfer_coeff;  // Energy transfer coefficient\n    val_t external_flow;   // External energy source/sink\n};\n\n// Static connectivity information for each element\nstruct ElementStatic {\n    idx_t material_idx;\n    idx_t num_connections;\n    idx_t connected_idx[MAX_CONNECTIONS];     // Indices of connected elements (local numbering)\n    val_t connected_flux[MAX_CONNECTIONS];    // Flux coefficients for each connection\n};\n\n// Dynamic state for each element\nstruct ElementDynamic {\n    val_t current_energy;\n    val_t total_flux;\n};\n\n// Halo exchange pattern: which owned elements to send to / ghosts to receive from each neighbor rank\nstruct HaloExchange {\n    std::vector<int> send_ranks;                   // Neighbor ranks we send to\n    std::vector<int> send_offsets;                 // Prefix offsets into send_local_idx (size+1)\n    std::vector<idx_t> send_local_idx;             // Local indices of owned elements to send\n    std::vector<val_t> send_buf;                   // Packed send std::vector<MPI_Request> requests;             // Persistent requests (recv then send)\n};\n\n// World state (distributed): each rank owns a contiguous range of global element indices\nstruct World {\n    std::vector<Material> materials;\n    std::vector<ElementStatic> elements_static;        // Owned elements only\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic;      // Owned elements followed by ghost elements\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic_swap;\n\n    idx_t n_global = 0;        // Total number of elements across all ranks\n    idx_t global_begin = 0;    // First global index owned by this rank\n    idx_t n_owned = 0;         // Number of owned elements\n    idx_t n_ghost = 0;         // Number of ghost elements\n\n    std::vector<idx_t> interior_elems;   // Owned elements with only owned neighbors\n    std::vector<idx_t> boundary_elems;   // Owned elements with at least one ghost neighbor\n\n    HaloExchange halo;\n};\n\n// Material type IDs\nconstexpr idx_t DEFAULT_MAT_ID = 0;\nconstexpr idx_t INFLOW_MAT_ID = 1;\nconstexpr idx_t OUTFLOW_MAT_ID = 2;\n\n// Contiguous block partitioning of [0, n_global) over ranks\ninline idx_t partitionBegin(idx_t n_global, int size, int rank) {\n    return (n_global * static_cast<idx_t>(rank)) / static_cast<idx_t>(size);\n}\n\ninline int ownerOf(idx_t gidx, idx_t n_global, int size) {\n    // Initial guess from proportional mapping, then correct for rounding\n    int r = static_cast<int>((static_cast<__uint128_t>(gidx) * static_cast<idx_t>(size)) / n_global);\n    if (r >= size) r = size - 1;\n    while (r > 0 && partitionBegin(n_global, size, r) > gidx) --r;\n    while (r + 1 < size && partitionBegin(n_global, size, r + 1) <= gidx) ++r;\n    return r;\n}\n\n// Build the halo exchange pattern from the (global-indexed) connectivity of the owned elements,\n// renumber connectivity to local indices, and classify owned elements as interior/boundary.\nvoid setupHalo(World& world, MPI_Comm comm) {\n    int rank, size;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(comm, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(comm, &size);\n\n    const idx_t gbegin = world.global_begin;\n    const idx_t gend = gbegin + world.n_owned;\n\n    // Collect ghost global indices per owner rank\n    std::vector<std::vector<idx_t>> ghost_by_rank(size);\n    for (idx_t i = 0; i < world.n_owned; ++i) {\n        const ElementStatic& es = world.elements_static[i];\n        for (idx_t j = 0; j < es.num_connections; ++j) {\n            const idx_t g = es.connected_idx[j];\n            if (g < gbegin || g >= gend) {\n                ghost_by_rank[ownerOf(g, world.n_global, size)].push_back(g);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Sorted unique ghost list per rank; assign ghost local indices in rank order\n    HaloExchange& h = world.halo;\n    std::unordered_map<idx_t, idx_t> ghost_local;  // global -> local index\n    idx_t next_local = world.n_owned;\n    h.recv_offsets.push_back(0);\n    std::vector<int> recv_counts(size, 0);\n    std::vector<idx_t> recv_global_all;\n    for (int r = 0; r < size; ++r) {\n        auto& v = ghost_by_rank[r];\n        if (v.empty()) continue;\n        std::sort(v.begin(), v.end());\n        v.erase(std::unique(v.begin(), v.end()), v.end());\n        h.recv_ranks.push_back(r);\n        for (idx_t g : v) {\n            ghost_local.emplace(g, next_local++);\n            recv_global_all.push_back(g);\n        }\n        recv_counts[r] = static_cast<int>(v.size());\n        h.recv_offsets.push_back(static_cast<int>(recv_global_all.size()));\n    }\n    world.n_ghost = next_local - world.n_owned;\n\n    // Tell owners which of their elements we need\n    std::vector<int> send_counts(size, 0);\n    MPI_Alltoall(recv_counts.data(), 1, MPI_INT, send_counts.data(), 1, MPI_INT, comm);\n\n    std::vector<int> recv_displs(size + 1, 0), send_displs(size + 1, 0);\n    for (int r = 0; r < size; ++r) {\n        recv_displs[r + 1] = recv_displs[r] + recv_counts[r];\n        send_displs[r + 1] = send_displs[r] + send_counts[r];\n    }\n    std::vector<idx_t> send_global_all(send_displs[size]);\n    MPI_Alltoallv(recv_global_all.data(), recv_counts.data(), recv_displs.data(), MPI_UINT64_T,\n                  send_global_all.data(), send_counts.data(), send_displs.data(), MPI_UINT64_T, comm);\n\n    h.send_offsets.push_back(0);\n    for (int r = 0; r < size; ++r) {\n        if (send_counts[r] == 0) continue;\n        h.send_ranks.push_back(r);\n        for (int k = send_displs[r]; k < send_displs[r + 1]; ++k) {\n            h.send_local_idx.push_back(send_global_all[k] - gbegin);\n        }\n        h.send_offsets.push_back(static_cast<int>(h.send_local_idx.size()));\n    }\n    h.send_buf.resize(h.send_local_idx.size());\n\n    // Renumber connectivity to local indices and classify elements\n    world.interior_elems.reserve(world.n_owned);\n    for (idx_t i = 0; i < world.n_owned; ++i) {\n        ElementStatic& es = world.elements_static[i];\n        bool has_ghost = false;\n        for (idx_t j = 0; j < es.num_connections; ++j) {\n            const idx_t g = es.connected_idx[j];\n            if (g >= gbegin && g < gend) {\n                es.connected_idx[j] = g - gbegin;\n            } else {\n                es.connected_idx[j] = ghost_local[g];\n                has_ghost = true;\n            }\n        }\n        if (has_ghost) world.boundary_elems.push_back(i);\n        else world.interior_elems.push_back(i);\n    }\n\n    // Resize dynamic arrays to include ghosts (ghosts initialized to zero energy)\n    world.elements_dynamic.resize(world.n_owned + world.n_ghost, ElementDynamic{0.0, 0.0});\n    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(world.n_owned + world.n_ghost, ElementDynamic{0.0, 0.0});\n}\n\n// Build a 2D square grid as an unstructured mesh (local portion of a block partition)\n// This represents computation on arbitrarily-shaped geometries\nvoid buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root, MPI_Comm comm) {\n    int rank, size;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(comm, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(comm, &size);\n\n    const idx_t n_elems = static_cast<idx_t>(n_elems_root) * static_cast<idx_t>(n_elems_root);\n    world.n_global = n_elems;\n    world.global_begin = partitionBegin(n_elems, size, rank);\n    world.n_owned = partitionBegin(n_elems, size, rank + 1) - world.global_begin;\n\n    // Initialize materials\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0});    // Default material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material\n\n    // Allocate owned elements\n    world.elements_static.resize(world.n_owned);\n    world.elements_dynamic.resize(world.n_owned);\n    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(world.n_owned);\n\n    // Initialize all owned elements with default material and zero energy\n    for (idx_t i = 0; i < world.n_owned; ++i) {\n        world.elements_static[i].material_idx = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;\n        world.elements_static[i].num_connections = 0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n    }\n\n    // Build connectivity (global indices): each element connects to its neighbors in 2D grid\n    const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};\n    for (idx_t i = 0; i < world.n_owned; ++i) {\n        const idx_t gidx = world.global_begin + i;\n        const int x = static_cast<int>(gidx / n_elems_root);\n        const int y = static_cast<int>(gidx % n_elems_root);\n        ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[i];\n\n        // Connect to neighbors (up, down, left, right)\n        for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {\n            const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];\n            const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];\n\n            // Check if neighbor is within bounds\n            if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 && ny < n_elems_root) {\n                const idx_t neighbor_idx = static_cast<idx_t>(nx) * n_elems_root + ny;\n                elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;\n                elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;\n                elem.num_connections++;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics\n    const idx_t last = n_elems_root - 1;\n    auto setMaterial = [&](idx_t gidx, idx_t mat) {\n        if (gidx >= world.global_begin && gidx < world.global_begin + world.n_owned) {\n            world.elements_static[gidx - world.global_begin].material_idx = mat;\n        }\n    };\n    setMaterial(0 * n_elems_root + 0, INFLOW_MAT_ID);\n    setMaterial(0 * n_elems_root + last, OUTFLOW_MAT_ID);\n    setMaterial(last * n_elems_root + 0, OUTFLOW_MAT_ID);\n    setMaterial(last * n_elems_root + last, INFLOW_MAT_ID);\n\n    setupHalo(world, comm);\n}\n\n// Compute energy flux between two elements\ninline val_t computeFlux(const Material& mat, const ElementDynamic& this_elem,\n                        val_t connection_flux, const ElementDynamic& other_elem) {\n    return (other_elem.current_energy - this_elem.current_energy) *\n           mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;\n}\n\n// Update a single owned element (reads elements_dynamic, writes elements_dynamic_swap)\ninline void updateElement(World& world, const idx_t i) {\n    const ElementStatic& elem_static = world.elements_static[i];\n    const ElementDynamic& elem_dyn = world.elements_dynamic[i];\n    const Material& mat = world.materials[elem_static.material_idx];\n\n    // Start with external flow\n    val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;\n\n    // Add flux from all connected elements\n    for (idx_t j = 0; j < elem_static.num_connections; ++j) {\n        const idx_t neighbor_idx = elem_static.connected_idx[j];\n        const ElementDynamic& neighbor_dyn = world.elements_dynamic[neighbor_idx];\n        total_flux += computeFlux(mat, elem_dyn, elem_static.connected_flux[j], neighbor_dyn);\n    }\n\n    // Update element state\n    ElementDynamic& elem_write = world.elements_dynamic_swap[i];\n    elem_write.current_energy = elem_dyn.current_energy + total_flux;\n    elem_write.total_flux = elem_dyn.total_flux + std::abs(total_flux);\n}\n\n// Ghost current_energy values live in the dynamic arrays after the owned elements; exchange them\n// using the derived datatype (stride of ElementDynamic) directly into/out of the given buffer.\nvoid startHaloExchange(World& world, MPI_Comm comm) {\n    HaloExchange& h = world.halo;\n    const int n_recv = static_cast<int>(h.recv_ranks.size());\n    const int n_send = static_cast<int>(h.send_ranks.size());\n    h.requests.resize(n_recv + n_send);\n\n    // Pack send buffer with owned current_energy values\n    const ElementDynamic* dyn = world.elements_dynamic.data();\n    for (size_t k = 0; k < h.send_local_idx.size(); ++k) {\n        h.send_buf[k] = dyn[h.send_local_idx[k]].current_energy;\n    }\n\n    // Ghost energies are received into a contiguous scratch region of the swap array's ghost\n    // slots? No: they must land in elements_dynamic (the read buffer). Receive into a strided\n    // location with a derived type to avoid an unpack pass.\n    static MPI_Datatype ghost_type = MPI_DATATYPE_NULL;\n    if (ghost_type == MPI_DATATYPE_NULL) {\n        MPI_Datatype tmp;\n        MPI_Type_create_resized(MPI_DOUBLE, 0, sizeof(ElementDynamic), &tmp);\n        MPI_Type_commit(&tmp);\n        ghost_type = tmp;\n    }\n\n    for (int k = 0; k < n_recv; ++k) {\n        const int count = h.recv_offsets[k + 1] - h.recv_offsets[k];\n        MPI_Irecv(&world.elements_dynamic[world.n_owned + h.recv_offsets[k]].current_energy, count,\n                  ghost_type, h.recv_ranks[k], 0, comm, &h.requests[k]);\n    }\n    for (int k = 0; k < n_send; ++k) {\n        const int count = h.send_offsets[k + 1] - h.send_offsets[k];\n        MPI_Isend(h.send_buf.data() + h.send_offsets[k], count, MPI_DOUBLE, h.send_ranks[k], 0, comm,\n                  &h.requests[n_recv + k]);\n    }\n}\n\nvoid finishHaloExchange(World& world) {\n    HaloExchange& h = world.halo;\n    if (!h.requests.empty()) {\n        MPI_Waitall(static_cast<int>(h.requests.size()), h.requests.data(), MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);\n    }\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters, MPI_Comm comm) {\n    for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {\n        // Start exchanging ghost energies for this iteration\n        startHaloExchange(world, comm);\n\n        // Update interior elements while communication is in flight\n        for (const idx_t i : world.interior_elems) {\n            updateElement(world, i);\n        }\n\n        // Wait for ghosts, then update boundary elements\n        finishHaloExchange(world);\n        for (const idx_t i : world.boundary_elems) {\n            updateElement(world, i);\n        }\n\n        // Swap buffers\n        std::swap(world.elements_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic_swap);\n    }\n}\n\n// Validate simulation results (on the gathered global element array)\nbool validateResults(const std::vector<ElementDynamic>& elements) {\n    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n\n    for (const auto& elem : elements) {\n        energy_sum += elem.current_energy;\n        flux_sum += elem.total_flux;\n        energy_max = std::max(elem.current_energy, energy_max);\n        energy_min = std::min(elem.current_energy, energy_min);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Validation }\n\n    if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {\n        printf(\"  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\\n\");\n        // Don't fail validation as this can happen with external flows\n    }\n\n    if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Compute a simple hash of the results for verification.\n// Computed locally over owned elements using global indices; the per-element terms are combined\n// with XOR, so partial hashes are reduced across ranks with MPI_BXOR to obtain the exact serial hash.\nuint64_t computeHash(const std::vector<ElementDynamic>& elements, const idx_t n_owned, const idx_t global_begin) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    for (idx_t li = 0; li < n_owned; ++li) {\n        const idx_t i = global_begin + li;\n        // Simple hash combining energy and flux values\n        const uint64_t* e_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[li].current_energy);\n        const uint64_t* f_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[li].total_flux);\n        hash ^= (*e_ptr + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\n        hash ^= (*f_ptr + i) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;\n    }\n    return hash;\n}\n\n// Gather the owned dynamic state of all ranks into a global array on rank 0\nstd::vector<ElementDynamic> gatherDynamic(const World& world, MPI_Comm comm) {\n    int rank, size;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(comm, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(comm, &size);\n\n    std::vector<int> connections per element (for a 2D grid: 4 neighbors)\nconstexpr int MAX_CONNECTIONS = 8;\n\n// Material properties for energy transfer\nstruct Material {\n    val_t transfer_coeff;  // Energy transfer // Initial guess from proportional mapping, then correct for rounding\n    int r = static_cast<int>((static_cast<__uint128_t>(gidx) * static_cast<idx_t>(size)) / n_global);\n    if (r >= size) r = size - 1;\n    while (r > 0 && partitionBegin(n_global, size, r) > gidx) --r;\n    while (r + 1 < size && partitionBegin(n_global, size, r + 1) <= gidx) ++r;\n    return r;\n}\n\n// Build the halo exchange pattern from the (global-indexed) connectivity of the owned elements,\n// renumber connectivity to local indices, and classify owned elements as interior/boundary.\nvoid setupHalo(World& world, MPI_Comm comm) {\n    int rank, size;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(comm, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(comm, &size);\n\n    const idx_t gbegin = world.global_begin;\n    const idx_t gend = gbegin +               ghost_by_rank[ownerOf(g, world.n_global, size)].push_back(g);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Sorted unique ghost list per rank; assign ghost local indices in rank order\n    HaloExchange& h = world.halo;\n    std::unordered_map<idx_t, idx_t> ghost_local;  // global -> local index\n    idx_t next_local = world.n_owned;\n    h.recv_offsets.push_back(0);\n    std::vector<int> recv_counts(size, 0);\n    std::vector<idx_t> recv_global_all;\n    for (int r = 0; r < size; ++r) {\n        auto& v = ghost_by_rank[r];\n        if (v.empty()) continue;\n        std::sort(v.begin(), v.end());\n        v.erase(std::unique(v.begin(), v.end()), v.end());\n        h.recv_ranks.push_back(r);\n        for (idx_t g : v) {\n            ghost_local.emplace(g, next_local++);\n            recv_global_all.push_back(g);\n        }\n        recv_counts[r] = static_cast<int>(v.size());\n        h.recv_offsets.push_back(static_cast<int>(recv_global_all.size()));\n    }\n    world.n_ghost = next_local - world.n_owned;\n\n    // Tell owners which of their elements we need\n    std::vector<int> h.send_offsets.push_back(static_cast<int>(h.send_local_idx.size()));\n    }\n    h.send_buf.resize(h.send_local_idx.size());\n\n    // Renumber connectivity to local indices and classify elements\n block partition)\n// This represents computation on arbitrarily-shaped geometries\nvoid buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root, MPI_Comm comm) {\n    int rank, size;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(comm, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(comm, &size);\n\n    const idx_t n_elems = static_cast<idx_t>(n_elems_root) * static_cast<idx_t>(n_elems_root);\n    world.n_global = n_elems;\n    world.global_begin = partitionBegin(n_elems, size, rank);\n    world.n_owned = partitionBegin(n_elems, size, rank + 1) - world.global_begin;\n\n    // Initialize materials\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0});    // Default material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material\n\n    // Allocate owned elements\n    world.elements_static.resize(world.n_owned);\n    world.elements_dynamic.resize(world.n_owned);\n    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(world.n_owned);\n\n    // Initialize all owned elements with default material and zero energy\n    for (idx_t i = 0; i < world.n_owned; ++i) {\n        world.elements_static[i].material_idx = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;\n        world.elements_static[i].num_connections = 0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n    }\n\n    // Build connectivity (global indices): each element connects to its neighbors in 2D grid\n    const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, static_cast<int>(gidx % n_elems_root);\n        ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[i];\n\n        // Connect to neighbors (up, down, left, right)\n        for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {\n            const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];\n            const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];\n\n            // Check if neighbor is within bounds\n            if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 && ny < n_elems_root) {\n                const idx_t neighbor_idx = static_cast<idx_t>(nx) * n_elems_root + ny;\n                elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;\n                elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;\n                elem.num_connections++;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics\n    const idx_t last = n_elems_root - 1;\n    auto setMaterial = [&](idx_t gidx, idx_t mat) {\n        if (gidx >= world.global_begin && gidx < world.global_begin + world.n_owned) {\n            world.elements_static[gidx - world.global_begin].material_idx = mat;\n        }\n    };\n    setMaterial(0 * n_elems_root + 0, INFLOW_MAT_ID);\n    setMaterial(0 * n_elems_root + last, OUTFLOW_MAT_ID);\n    setMaterial(last * n_elems_root + 0, OUTFLOW_MAT_ID);\n    setMaterial(last * n_elems_root + last, INFLOW_MAT_ID);\n\n    setupHalo(world, comm);\n}\n\n// Compute energy flux between two elements\ninline val_t computeFlux(const Material& mat, const ElementDynamic& this_elem,\n                        val_t connection_flux, const ElementDynamic& other_elem) {\n    return (other_elem.current_energy - this_elem.current_energy) *\n           mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;\n}\n\n// Update a single owned element (reads elements_dynamic, writes elements_dynamic_swap)\ninline void updateElement(World& world, const idx_t i) {\n    const ElementStatic& elem_static = world.elements_static[i];\n    const ElementDynamic& elem_dyn = world.elements_dynamic[i];\n    const Material& mat = world.materials[elem_static.material_idx];\n\n    // Start with external flow\n    val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;\n\n    // Add flux from all connected elements\n    for (idx_t j = 0; j < elem_static.num_connections; ++j) {\n        const idx_t neighbor_idx = elem_static.connected_idx[j];\n        const ElementDynamic& neighbor_dyn = world.elements_dynamic[neighbor_idx];\n        total_flux += computeFlux(mat, elem_dyn, elem_static.connected_flux[j], neighbor_dyn);\n    }\n\n    // Update element state\n    ElementDynamic& elem_write = world.elements_dynamic_swap[i];\n    elem_write.current_energy = elem_dyn.current_energy + total_flux;\n    elem_write.total_flux = elem_dyn.total_flux + std::abs(total_flux);\n}\n\n// Ghost current_energy values live in the dynamic arrays after the owned elements; exchange them\n// using the derived datatype (stride of ElementDynamic) directly into/out of the given buffer.\nvoid startHaloExchange(World& world, MPI_Comm comm) {\n    HaloExchange& h = world.halo;\n    const int n_recv = static_cast<int>(h.recv_ranks.size());\n    const int n_send = static_cast<int>(h.send_ranks.size());\n    h.requests.resize(n_recv + n_send);\n\n    // Pack send buffer with owned current_energy values\n    const ElementDynamic* dyn = world.elements_dynamic.data();\n    for (size_t k = 0; k < h.send_local_idx.size(); ++k) {\n        h.send_buf[k] = dyn[h.send_local_idx[k]].current_energy;\n    }\n\n    // Ghost energies are received into a contiguous scratch region of the swap array's ghost\n    // slots? No: they must land in elements_dynamic (the read buffer). Receive into a strided\n    // location with a derived type to avoid an unpack pass.\n    static MPI_Datatype ghost_type = MPI_DATATYPE_NULL;\n    if (ghost_type == MPI_DATATYPE_NULL) {\n        MPI_Datatype tmp;\n        MPI_Type_create_resized(MPI_DOUBLE, 0, sizeof(ElementDynamic), &tmp);\n        MPI_Type_commit(&tmp);\n        ghost_type = tmp;\n    }\n\n    for (int k = 0; k < n_recv; ++k) {\n        const int count = h.recv_offsets[k + 1] - h.recv_offsets[k];\n        MPI_Irecv(&world.elements_dynamic[world.n_owned + h.recv_offsets[k]].current_energy, count,\n                  ghost_type, h.recv_ranks[k], 0, comm, &h.requests[k]);\n    }\n    for (int k = 0; k < n_send; ++k) {\n        const int count = h.send_offsets[k + 1] - h.send_offsets[k];\n        MPI_Isend(h.send_buf.data() + h.send_offsets[k], count, MPI_DOUBLE, h.send_ranks[k], 0, comm,\n                  &h.requests[n_recv + k]);\n    }\n}\n\nvoid finishHaloExchange(World& world) {\n    HaloExchange& h = world.halo;\n    if (!h.requests.empty()) {\n        MPI_Waitall(static_cast<int>(h.requests.size()), h.requests.data(), MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);\n    }\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters, MPI_Comm comm) {\n    for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {\n        // Start exchanging ghost energies for this iteration\n        startHaloExchange(world, comm);\n\n        // Update interior elements while communication is in flight\n        for (const idx_t i : world.interior_elems) {\n            updateElement(world, i);\n        }\n\n        // Wait for ghosts, then update boundary elements\n        finishHaloExchange(world);\n        for (const idx_t i : world.boundary_elems) {\n            updateElement(world, i);\n        }\n\n        // Swap buffers\n        std::swap(world.elements_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic_swap);\n    }\n}\n\n// Validate simulation results (on the gathered global element array)\nbool validateResults(const std::vector<ElementDynamic>& elements) {\n    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n\n    for (const auto& elem : elements) {\n        energy_sum += elem.current_energy;\n        flux_sum += elem.total_flux;\n        energy_max = std::max(elem.current_energy, energy_max);\n        energy_min = std::min(elem.current_energy, energy_min);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Validation results:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  Energy sum: %.12f\\n\", energy_sum);\n    printf(\"  Flux sum: %.2f\\n\", flux_sum);\n    printf(\"  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", energy_min, energy_max);\n\n    // Check for numerical issues\n    constexpr val_t energy_epsilon = 1e-8;\n\n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {\n        printf(\"  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\\n\");\n        // Don't fail validation as this can happen with external flows\n    }\n\n    if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Compute a simple hash of the results for verification.\n// Computed locally over owned elements using global indices; the per-element terms are combined\n// with XOR, so partial hashes are reduced across ranks with MPI_BXOR to obtain the exact serial hash.\nuint64_t computeHash(const std::vector<ElementDynamic>& elements, const idx_t n_owned, const idx_t global_begin) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    for (idx_t li = 0; li < n_owned; ++li) {\n        const idx_t i = global_begin + li;\n        // Simple hash combining energy and flux values\n        const uint64_t* e_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[li].current_energy);\n        const uint64_t* f_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[li].total_flux);\n        hash ^= (*e_ptr + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\n        hash ^= (*f_ptr + i) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;\n    }\n    return hash;\n}\n\n// Gather the owned dynamic state of all ranks into a global array on rank partitionBegin(world.n_global, size, r)) * 2);\n        displs[r + 1] = displs[r] + counts[r];\n    }\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic> all;\n    if (rank == 0) all.resize(world.n_global);\n world.elements_dynamic[i];\n            const Material& mat = world.materials[elem_static.material_idx];\n            \n            // Start with external flow\n            val_t total_flux = elem_static.connected_idx[j];\n                const ElementDynamic& neighbor_dyn = world.elements_dynamic[neighbor_idx];\n                total_flux += computeFlux(mat, elem_dyn, elem_static.connected_flux[j], neighbor_dyn);\n            }\n            \n            // Update element state\n            ElementDynamic& elem_write = world.elements_dynamic_swap[i];\n            elem_write.current_energy = elem_dyn.current_energy + total_flux;\n            elem_write.total_flux = elem_dyn.total_flux + std::abs(total_flux);\n        }\n        \n        // Swap buffers\n       energy_sum += elem.current_energy;\n        flux_sum += elem.total_flux;\n        energy_max = std::max(elem.current_energy, energy_max);\n        energy_min = std::min(elem.current_energy, energy_min);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Validation results:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  Energy sum: %.12f\\n\", energy_sum);\n    printf(\"  Flux sum: %.2f\\n\", flux_sum);\n    printf(\"  Energy range: conservation)\\n\");\n        // Don't fail validation as this can happen with external flows\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Flux sum is not elements) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < elements.size(); ++i) {\n        // Simple hash combining energy and flux values\n        const uint64_t* e_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const hash ^= (*f_ptr + i) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;\n    }\n    return hash;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-fable-5-1","id":"msg_011Cfw8dSVa7cjs7RgpF33MV","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Done. The benchmark in `unstructured/unstructured.cpp` is now an MPI program, and its output is bitwise identical to the original across every configuration I tested.\n\n**What changed**\n\n- **Domain decomposition.** Each rank owns a contiguous block of global element indices and builds only its own part of the mesh. The connectivity still uses global indices during construction, so the approach works for any unstructured mesh, not just the grid.\n- **Connectivity-driven halo.** After construction, each rank discovers which off-rank elements it references, groups them by owner, and negotiates send lists with one Alltoallv. Ghost elements are appended after the owned ones and the connectivity is renumbered to local indices.\n- **Overlapped exchange per iteration.** Owned energies are packed and sent with nonblocking MPI. Ghost values land directly in the read buffer through a strided derived datatype, so there is no unpack pass. Interior elements, stored as contiguous ranges so the hot loop matches the original's codegen, are updated while messages are in flight. Boundary elements are updated after the wait.\n- **Exact results.** The per-element arithmetic and summation order are unchanged. The result hash is an XOR of per-element terms, so partial hashes are combined with a bitwise-XOR reduce and match the serial hash exactly. For the validation and results printouts, the state is gathered to rank 0 outside the timed region and the original functions run unchanged, so sums and Kahan hashes are identical.\n- **Timing and output.** Barriers bracket the timed loop, and only rank 0 prints. The reported memory usage is now summed over ranks and includes ghost storage, so it is slightly higher than the serial figure.\n- **CMake** now requires MPI and links the target against `MPI::MPI_CXX`. The executable name is unchanged, and no new files or dependencies were added.\n\n**A finding worth knowing.** A single MPI rank initially ran about a quarter slower than the serial binary even though the hot loop assembly was identical. The cause was the kernel's automatic NUMA balancing injecting hint page faults into the loop of the core-bound MPI process. The program now sets a local memory policy at startup through a raw system call, which opts the process out of that scanning. One rank then matches the serial time. I saved this host-specific detail to memory.\n\n**Verification.** Grid sizes 7, 512, and 1000 with validation and results printing match the serial reference exactly on 1, 2, 3, 5, 7, 8, 16, and 32 ranks. Edge cases with ranks that own zero elements also match. Strong scaling on a 4096 by 4096 grid with 100 iterations, hash constant throughout:\n\n| Ranks | Time (ms) |\n|---|---|\n| serial | 16137 |\n| 1 | 16482 |\n| 2 | 8963 |\n| 16 | 3933 |\n| 64 | 2343 |\n| 128 | 1153 |\n\nThe flat step from 4 to 8 ranks reflects memory bandwidth saturation on one socket when ranks are mapped by core, since each iteration streams the large per-element static records. That is inherent to the data layout, which I kept to preserve the original semantics."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1483,"cache_read_input_tokens":67998,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1483},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"6b0827f2-5c9f-4e68-9753-941272013e46","uuid":"1953d47f-6e15-4212-a502-298b7706febd","timestamp":"2026-10-11T21:07:51.029Z","request_id":"req_011Cfw8dRSpuBgWTBFf2PVCa"}
